start with direct stringification\n    rep: dict[str, str] = {k: \"\" if v is None else str(v) for k, v in data.items()}\n\n    # 참석자수: digits only\n    if \"참석자수\" in rep:\n        rep[\"참석자수\"] = re.sub(r\"\\D+\", \"\", rep[\"참석자수\"])\n\n    # 만족도: X.X점 (5.0점 만점)\n    raw_sat = data.get(\"만족도\")\n    score_str: str | None = None\n    if isinstance(raw_sat, (int, float)):\n        score_str = str(raw_sat)\n    elif raw_sat is not None:\n        m = re.search(r\"([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?)\", str(raw_sat))\n        if m:\n            score_str = m.group(1)\n\n    rep[\"만족도\"] = f\"{score_str if score_str is not None else ''}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n\n    # 종합의견: append sentence\n    suffix = \"후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n    opinion = str(data.get(\"종합의견\") or \"\")\n    if opinion.rstrip().endswith(suffix):\n        rep[\"종합의견\"] = opinion\n    else:\n        sep = \"\" if (not opinion) or opinion.endswith((\" \", \"\\t\", \"\\n\")) else \" \"\n        rep[\"종합의견\"] = f\"{opinion}{sep}{suffix}\" if opinion else suffix\n\n    return rep\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders(text: str, rep: dict[str, str]) -> str:\n    # Replace all {{키}} patterns in the given text.\n    def _sub(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in rep:\n            raise KeyError(f\"Unknown placeholder: {key}\")\n        return rep[key]\n\n    return re.sub(r\"\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}\", _sub, text)\n\n\ndef _remove_layout_cache_within(ancestor: ET.Element, parent_map: dict[ET.Element, ET.Element]) -> int:\n    removed = 0\n    # climb to find enclosing run and p\n            cur = el\n            run_anc = None\n            p_anc = None\n            while cur in parent_map:\n                cur = parent_map[cur]\n                ln = _localname(cur.tag)\n                if run_anc is None and ln == \"run\":\n                    run_anc = cur\n                if p_anc is None and ln == \"p\":\n                    p_anc = cur\n                if run_anc is not None and p_anc is not None:\n                    break\n\n            if run_anc is not None:\n                modified_runs.add(run_anc)\n            if p_anc is not None:\n                modified_ps.add(p_anc)\n\n    #
